# #100 (5a452fa), the allocation-free SPECIES_MAX finish, so the finishMax rows of both runs time
# the finish that writes out its first eight lanes with constant indices, where the 21 November
# file's boxed on mixed data; and #83 (73703ef), expm1Core, the core entry sinh calls, which
# Expm1Benchmark.java's new core rows time. pom.xml moved JUnit, Surefire and other plugin versions

# options include -foe true, so a row that failed would have failed the run; every row of
